    console.log(`✓ Dashboard running at http://0.0.0.0:${port}`);
  });
}
===== ./sub.js =====
import { SubBots } from "meowsab";
import { Level } from "level";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

async function sub(client) {
  global.subBots = new SubBots(client.commandSystem);

  SubBots.pariCode("KAIT1234");

  const { config } = client;

  await global.subBots.setConfig({
    commandsPath: config.commandsPath || "./plugins",
    owners: config.owners,
    prefix: config.prefix,
    info: config.info,
    printQR: false,
  });

  global.subBots.on("error", (uid, error) => {
    console.error(`❌ [SubBot ${uid}] Error:`, error?.message || error);
  });

  const subDir = path.resolve("sub");
  const botConfigs = [];

  if (fs.existsSync(subDir)) {
    for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(subDir)) {
      if (!/^shard-/.test(entry)) continue;
      const shardPath = path.join(subDir, entry);

      const lockFile = path.join(shardPath, "LOCK");
      if (fs.existsSync(lockFile)) fs.rmSync(lockFile);

      let db;
      try {
        db = new Level(shardPath, { valueEncoding: "utf8" });
        await db.open();
        for await (const [key, value] of db.iterator()) {
          if (key.endsWith("::phone")) {
            botConfigs.push({
              uid: key.slice(0, -"::phone".length),
              phone: value.replace(/^\+/, ""),
            });
          }
        }
      } catch (readErr) {
        console.error(
          `⚠️ Failed to scan shard ${entry}:`,
          readErr?.message || readErr,
        );
      } finally {
        if (db) {
          try {
            await db.close();
          } catch {}
        }
      }
    }
  }

  let ver, gCfg;
  try {
    [ver, gCfg] = await Promise.all([
      global.subBots.ver_(),
      global.subBots.gCfg_(),
    ]);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(
      "❌ [SubBots] Failed to initialise load parameters:",
      err?.message || err,
    );
    return global.subBots;
  }

  global.subBotRegistry = new Map();

  const reconnectInProgress = new Set();

  let successCount = 0;
  let failedCount = 0;
  for (const { uid, phone } of botConfigs) {
    try {
      await global.subBots.spawn_(uid, phone, gCfg, ver);
      global.subBotRegistry.set(uid, phone);
      successCount++;
    } catch (error) {
      failedCount++;
      console.error(
        `❌ [SubBot ${uid}] Failed to load (skipping):`,
        error?.message || error,
      );
    }
  }
  console.log(
    `✅ SubBots load complete — ${successCount} succeeded, ${failedCount} failed`,
  );

  global.subBots.on("ready", async (uid, sock) => {
    console.log(`✅ [SubBot ${uid}] Connected!`);
  });

  global.subBots.on("pair", (uid, code) => {
    console.log(`🔐 [SubBot ${uid}] Pairing code: ${code}`);
  });

  global.subBots.on("message", async (uid, msg) => {
    if (msg.key.id.includes("3EB0")) return;

    const bot = global.subBots.get(uid);
    const sock = bot?.sock;
    if (!sock) return;

    const chatJid = msg.key.remoteJid;
    const isGroup = chatJid.endsWith("@g.us");

    if (isGroup) {
      const isVcard =
        msg.message?.contactMessage || msg.message?.contactsArrayMessage;
      if (isVcard) {
        try {
          const groupMetadata = await sock.groupMetadata(chatJid);
          const participants = groupMetadata.participants;
          const sender = msg.key.participant || msg.participant;

          const isSenderAdmin = participants.find(
            (p) => p.id === sender,
          )?.admin;
          const isBotAdmin = participants.find(
            (p) => p.id === sock.user.id.split(":")[0] + "@s.whatsapp.net",
          )?.admin;

          if (!isSenderAdmin && isBotAdmin) {
            await sock.groupParticipantsUpdate(chatJid, [sender], "remove");
            await sock.sendMessage(chatJid, {
              text: `🚨 @${sender.split("@")[0]}`,
              mentions: [sender],
            });
            return;
          }
        } catch (e) {
          console.error(e);
        }
      }
    }

    const body = getMessageText(msg);
    if (!body) return;

    try {
      if (body === "تست") {
        await sock.sendMessage(chatJid, {
          react: { text: "✅", key: msg.key },
        });
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`❌ [SubBot ${uid}] Send error:`, error?.message || error);
    }
  });

  const MAX_RETRIES = 3;
  const BASE_DELAY_MS = 5000;

  async function attemptReconnect(uid, attempt) {
    const phone = global.subBotRegistry.get(uid);
    if (!phone) {
      console.error(
        `❌ [SubBot ${uid}] Cannot reconnect — phone not found in registry`,
      );
      reconnectInProgress.delete(uid);
      return;
    }

    if (attempt > MAX_RETRIES) {
      console.error(
        `❌ [SubBot ${uid}] Permanent failure — gave up after ${MAX_RETRIES} reconnect attempts`,
      );
      reconnectInProgress.delete(uid);
      return;
    }

    const delay = BASE_DELAY_MS * Math.pow(2, attempt - 1);
    console.log(
      `🔄 [SubBot ${uid}] Reconnect attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_RETRIES} in ${delay / 1000}s…`,
    );

    setTimeout(async () => {
      try {
        await global.subBots.spawn_(uid, phone, gCfg, ver);
        console.log(
          `✅ [SubBot ${uid}] Reconnected successfully (attempt ${attempt})`,
        );
        reconnectInProgress.delete(uid);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(
          `⚠️ [SubBot ${uid}] Reconnect attempt ${attempt} failed:`,
          err?.message || err,
        );
        await attemptReconnect(uid, attempt + 1);
      }
    }, delay);
  }

  global.subBots.on("close", (uid) => {
    console.log(`🔌 [SubBot ${uid}] Disconnected`);
    if (reconnectInProgress.has(uid)) {
      console.log(
        `⏩ [SubBot ${uid}] Reconnect already in progress, skipping duplicate`,
      );
      return;
    }
    reconnectInProgress.add(uid);
    attemptReconnect(uid, 1);
  });

  global.subBots.on("badSession", (uid) => {
    console.log(`⚠️ [SubBot ${uid}] Bad session, removed`);
  });

  return global.subBots;
}

function getMessageText(msg) {
  if (!msg.message) return null;
  if (msg.message.conversation) return msg.message.conversation;
  if (msg.message.extendedTextMessage?.text)
    return msg.message.extendedTextMessage.text;
  if (msg.message.imageMessage?.caption)
    return msg.message.imageMessage.caption;
  if (msg.message.videoMessage?.caption)
    return msg.message.videoMessage.caption;
  return msg.body || null;
}

export default sub;
===== ./index.js =====
import { Client } from "meowsab";
import { group, access } from "./system/control.js";
import UltraDB from "./system/UltraDB.js";
import sub from "./sub.js";
import { startDashboard, state, pushUpdate } from "./dashboard.js";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
global.isPublic = true;

/* =========== Dashboard ========== */
startDashboard();

/* =========== Re-pairing retry config ========== */
const configuredRepairAttempts = Number.parseInt(
  process.env.MAX_REPAIR_ATTEMPTS ?? "3",
  10,
);
const MAX_REPAIR_ATTEMPTS =
  Number.isInteger(configuredRepairAttempts) && configuredRepairAttempts >= 1
    ? configuredRepairAttempts
    : 3;
if (MAX_REPAIR_ATTEMPTS !== configuredRepairAttempts) {
  console.warn(
    `[Bot] Invalid MAX_REPAIR_ATTEMPTS value; using ${MAX_REPAIR_ATTEMPTS}. Set it to a positive integer.`,
  );
}
let repairAttempts = 0;

/* =========== Intercept stdout to capture pairing code ========== */
const _stripAnsi = (str) =>
  str.replace(/\x1B\[[0-9;]*[mGKHF]/g, "").replace(/\x1B\][^\x07]*\x07/g, "");
const _origWrite = process.stdout.write.bind(process.stdout);
process.stdout.write = (chunk, ...rest) => {
  const raw = typeof chunk === "string" ? chunk : chunk.toString();
  const line = _stripAnsi(raw);
  const match = line.match(/Pairing\s*Code\s+([A-Z0-9]{4,})/i);
  if (match) {
    state.pairingCode = match[1];
    state.status = "connecting";
    pushUpdate();
  }
  if (/Session logged out/i.test(line)) {
    if (repairAttempts >= MAX_REPAIR_ATTEMPTS) {
      state.status = "failed";
      state.pairingCode = null;
      state.repairAttempts = repairAttempts;
      pushUpdate();
      console.error(
        `[Bot] Re-pairing limit reached (${MAX_REPAIR_ATTEMPTS} attempts). Giving up to prevent endless restart loop.`,
      );
    } else {
      repairAttempts += 1;
      const sessionDir = path.resolve("./session");
      fs.rmSync(sessionDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
      fs.mkdirSync(sessionDir, { recursive: true });
      state.status = "re-pairing";
      state.pairingCode = null;
      state.repairAttempts = repairAttempts;
      pushUpdate();
      console.log(
        `[Bot] Re-pairing attempt ${repairAttempts}/${MAX_REPAIR_ATTEMPTS}...`,
      );
      setTimeout(() => {
        client.start();
      }, 1500);
    }
  }
  return _origWrite(chunk, ...rest);
};

/* =========== Wipe stale session on exit ========== */
function wipeSession() {
  try {
    const sessionDir = path.resolve("./session");
    fs.rmSync(sessionDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
    fs.mkdirSync(sessionDir, { recursive: true });
  } catch (_) {}
}

for (const sig of ["SIGINT", "SIGTERM", "SIGHUP"]) {
  process.on(sig, () => {
    wipeSession();
    process.exit(0);
  });
}

/* =========== Validate session before connecting ========== */
(function validateSession() {
  const credsPath = path.resolve("./session/creds.json");
  try {
    if (!fs.existsSync(credsPath)) return;
    const raw = fs.readFileSync(credsPath, "utf8").trim();
    if (!raw) throw new Error("empty");
    JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch (_) {
    console.log(
      "[Bot] Stale or invalid session detected — wiping before connect.",
    );
    wipeSession();
  }
})();

/* =========== Reset-pairing hook (callable from plugins) ========== */
global.resetPairing = () => {
  if (state.status !== "failed") return false;
  repairAttempts = 0;
  state.repairAttempts = 0;
  wipeSession();
  state.status = "re-pairing";
  state.pairingCode = null;
  pushUpdate();
  console.log("[Bot] Owner triggered reset-pairing — retrying...");
  setTimeout(() => client.start(), 1500);
  return true;
};

/* =========== Start ========== */
client.start();

setTimeout(async () => {
  if (client.commandSystem) {
    const cmds = client.commandSystem.getAll();
    state.commandCount = cmds.length;
    pushUpdate();
    sub(client);
  }
}, 2000);

/* =========== Catch Errors ========== */
process.on("uncaughtException", (e) => {
  if (e.message.includes("rate-overlimit")) return;
  console.error("Uncaught Exception:", e.message);
  wipeSession();
  process.exit(1);
});

process.on("unhandledRejection", (err) => {
  console.error("Unhandled Rejection:", err);
  wipeSession();
});
